## Details

Manages the trust framework, defining identities (Humans, Agents, Teams) and enforcing runtime policies and access control via the Diary provenance system.

### Trust & Provenance Controller
The core engine responsible for identity definition, policy enforcement, and the append-only provenance ledger. It manages the lifecycle of RuntimePolicy and RuntimeProfile, ensuring that agents operate within defined guardrails. The Diary component acts as the ground truth for all interactions, creating a verifiable audit trail of identity → action → proof.

### Agent Output & Contract Validator
This component manages the Proof phase of the Governance loop. It validates that agent outputs adhere to specific contracts and ensures that the resulting artifacts (Context Packs or final outputs) do not leak private workspace data or violate distribution constraints. It acts as the gatekeeper between agent execution and the persistence of the result into the provenance system.


**Related Classes/Methods**:

- `libs.agent-runtime.src.output-tools.SubmitOutputContract`:33-52
- `tools.src.check-pack.checkPackage`:207-260
- `libs.agent-runtime.src.subagent-output-contracts.SubagentContractRegistry`:50-55
- `tools.src.check-pack.checkNoPrivateWorkspaceDeps`:153-165

### Agent Key & Identity Service
Provides the cryptographic foundation for agent identities. It manages the issuance, rotation, and revocation of agent-specific keys used to sign provenance entries and authenticate requests. This service ensures that identity is cryptographically decoupled from the LLM, allowing for secure, verifiable identity bindings.


**Related Classes/Methods**:

- `libs.agent-key-service.src.agent-key-service.AgentKey`:34-47
- `libs.agent-key-service.src.agent-key-service.AgentKeyBinding`:63-66
- `libs.agent-key-service.src.agent-key-service.RotateAgentKeyInput`:113-119
- `libs.agent-key-service.src.agent-key-service.RevokeAgentKeyInput`:121-129
